### [214] [GRACE:Gradient-guided Coreset Selection for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28361)
*Praveen Bushipaka,Andrea D'Angelo,Lucia Passaro,Tommaso Cucinotta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GRACE方法，通过梯度引导的共核集选择，从异构语料中自动推断遗忘和保留集，以解决大语言模型在仅有少量不良行为示例时的机器遗忘问题，在保持遗忘质量的同时显著提升模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型语言模型（LLM）机器遗忘方法通常假设预先指定了遗忘集和保留集。但在现实场景中，请求往往只提供少数不良行为的示例，需要从异构语料库中推断出遗忘和保留集，这一数据选择问题亟待解决。

Method: 提出GRACE，一种梯度引导的共核集选择方法。首先，从诱发不良行为的种子示例中计算遗忘方向；其次，使用非负正交匹配追踪（OMP）选择近似该方向的紧凑遗忘共核集；最后，为了保持模型效用，在投影掉遗忘方向后的剩余梯度空间中应用聚类正交匹配追踪来选择保留示例。

Result: 在两个目标领域、两种模型家族和四种遗忘算法上进行了评估。GRACE在保持可比拟的遗忘质量的同时提高了模型效用，特别是在与先前的基于梯度的选择方法相比时，表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: GRACE有效解决了从少量示例中推断遗忘/保留集的数据选择难题，为LLM机器遗忘提供了更高效且实用的解决方案，显著优化了遗忘效果与模型效用之间的平衡。

Abstract: Machine Unlearning methods for Large Language Models typically assume pre-specified forget and retain sets. In realistic settings, however, requests may provide only a few examples of undesired behavior, requiring forget and retain sets to be inferred from heterogeneous corpora. We study this data-selection problem and propose GRACE , a gradient-guided coreset selection method that constructs both forget and retain sets for LLM unlearning. GRACE first computes a forget direction from seed examples that elicit the undesired behavior, then selects a compact forget coreset whose gradients approximate this direction using non-negative orthogonal matching pursuit. To preserve model utility, it selects retain examples after projecting out the forget direction and applying clustered orthogonal matching pursuit in the remaining gradient space. Across two target domains, two model families, and four unlearning algorithms, GRACE improves model utility while maintaining comparable forget quality, with particularly consistent gains over prior gradient-based selection methods.

</details>


### [215] [EvoUndo: Recoverability-Constrained Self-Evolution for LLM Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28363)
*Tanmay Sah,Dolly Sah,Harshul Jain,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoUndo框架，解决LLM智能体自演化修改的不可逆问题。通过验证和恢复机制，在600个任务中发现197个失败案例，并证明结合精确状态定位与扩展恢复语言可显著提升恢复成功率至93%-99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体运行时自我修改（如提示词、工具等）虽能提升能力，但可能导致无法安全回滚的持久性副作用，特别是在不同于创建时的状态下。现有方法难以有效处理这些“自然失败”的恢复问题。

Method: 引入EvoUndo框架，用于表示、合成、诊断和独立验证模型生成的自我修改的可恢复性。使用确定性预言机分析，并通过协议锁定的2x2干预实验分离瓶颈：一是精确状态地址定位（grounding），二是恢复语言的表达能力（expressivity）。在gpt-oss-120b和Qwen3.8-27B上进行复现验证。

Result: 在600个未见任务中识别出197个能力提升但恢复失败的突变。原始恢复策略成功率为0/197。仅用恢复语言L0时，预言机恢复48/197；扩展恢复语言后提升至191/197。精确状态定位将原语言下的48例中的38例（79.2%）恢复成功；扩展语言使预言机定义S1层级的143例中142例（99.3%）恢复成功。在gpt-oss-120b上，增加精确定位至丰富语言使恢复率降至133/143（93.0%），该负面交互具有模型依赖性。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体自我演化需要协同设计验证机制、状态定位、见证语义和恢复语言表达能力，而非仅依赖迭代提示工程。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly modify their own prompts, tools, middleware, resources, and execution harnesses at runtime. Such self-evolution can improve capability, but a successful mutation may leave persistent effects that cannot be safely reversed in states different from the one in which it was created. We introduce EvoUndo, a framework for representing, synthesizing, diagnosing, and independently verifying recoverability of model-generated self-modifications across counterfactual states. Across 600 unseen one-shot self-evolution tasks, we identify 197 capability-improving mutations that fail recoverability verification. Under the original recovery representation, conventional repair strategies recover 0/197 of these natural failures. Deterministic oracle analysis recovers 48/197 under the original recovery language L0, while the extended recovery calculus increases empirical oracle recovery to 191/197. A protocol-locked 2x2 grounding-by-expressivity intervention then separates two bottlenecks: exact state-address grounding increases successful recovery from 0/48 to 38/48 (79.2%) when the original language is sufficient, while extending the recovery language enables recovery on 142/143 (99.3%) failures in the oracle-defined S1 stratum. On the primary gpt-oss-120b backbone, adding exact-address diagnostics to the richer language reduces recovery to 133/143 (93.0%); a Qwen3.8-27B replication preserves the grounding and expressivity effects but not this negative interaction, indicating that the latter is model-dependent. These results indicate that reliable agent self-evolution requires co-designing verification, state grounding, witness semantics, and recovery-language expressivity rather than relying on iterative prompting alone.

</details>


### [216] [MAP: A Benchmark on Multimodal Accessibility Planning for Real World Places](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28384)
*Jason Armitage,Ioannis Tsochantaridis,Linda Mazzone,Chuqiao Yan,Srini Narayanan,Sarah Ebling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAP基准，首个针对残障人士实地访问规划的多模态AI助手评估框架，包含主张验证和视觉证据检索两项新评估，支持动态数据刷新与自动/人工混合评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏专门针对具有无障碍需求的用户、在现实世界地点访问规划场景下评估多模态AI系统的基准测试。

Method: 构建MAP基准，包含两项新颖评估：1) 无障碍规划的主张验证（评估信息支持度及地点匹配度）；2) 无障碍规划的视觉证据检索（检查系统能否选择相关视觉证据）。方法论支持随时间变化的地点和无障碍信息对比，通过定期刷新真实标签数据实现，并结合自动评分与人工抽样评分。

Result: 成功建立并定义了MAP基准，提供了评估多模态AI系统在无障碍访问规划任务中表现的标准方法，包括对信息真实性验证和视觉证据选择能力的量化评估机制。

Conclusion: MAP为评估面向无障碍需求的多模态AI助手提供了首个标准化基准，其动态更新机制和混合评分体系有助于持续追踪和提升AI系统在现实世界复杂场景下的可靠性与实用性。

Abstract: We introduce MAP, the first benchmark to evaluate multimodal AI systems as assistants for users with accessibility requirements when planning visits to places in the real world. In our evaluation, systems are presented with requests to verify or recommend a point of interest meeting an accessibility requirement. MAP contains two novel assessments: Claim verification for accessibility planning assesses if information on places and stated accessibility features is supported and identifies places that satisfy requested accessibility features. Visual evidence retrieval for accessibility planning checks if a multimodal AI system can select visual evidence for the requested place and accessibility feature. Our methodology supports comparison of AI systems in a setting where place information and accessibility information can change over time by evaluating systems and refreshing ground truth data at scheduled times. The benchmark is based on automatic rating and human rating for a proportion of responses.

</details>


### [217] [Timing-Aware Repurchase Prediction for Web-Scale E-Commerce: Survival Models for Multi-Surface Grocery Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28393)
*Akshay Kekuda,Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan,Ishan Bhatt,Yanan Cao,Sinduja Subramaniam,Evren Korpeoglu,Kaushiki Nag,Kannan Achan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出用生存模型（Survival Models）替代电商中传统的二元分类重复购买推荐器，通过单一加速失效时间（AFT）模型预测直接回购时间。研究发现Log-Normal分布拟合效果最佳但Exponential AFT校准误差最低，实现了在减少模型数量的同时提升性能与校准质量的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电商回购推荐器通常被框架化为询问“客户是否会在W天内购买此商品”的二元问题，这需要对每个感兴趣的时间窗口单独训练一个模型，导致计算资源浪费和模型维护复杂。旨在寻找一种更高效、统一的建模方式来预测回购时间。

Method: 1. 使用生存模型直接预测回购时间，取代多 horizon 的二元分类器堆栈。
2. 在大型杂货电商平台数百万客户数据上进行评估，涵盖30多种消融配置。
3. 进行实证危险率分析，比较不同参数分布（如Weibull, Log-Normal, Exponential）的拟合度、排名能力和校准质量。
4. 采用单个体量更小的AFT模型替换多个分类器，并开发4参数参数化校准方法将原始生存CDF映射为各时间窗口的概率。

Result: 1. **危险率分析**：发现边际危险率略微递减（k ~ 0.9），反驳了“购买越久越可能回购”的直觉。Log-Normal分布具有最佳的边际拟合（R^2 = 0.998）和排名能力，而Weibull提供最佳的残差拟合，揭示了两者间的差异。
2. **模型效率与特征**：单个AFT模型可替代三个按时间窗口的二元分类器，匹配或超越其性能，且总树数量减少约3倍。生存目标改变了特征重要性：渠道节奏和近期性信号上升，聚合频率计数下降。
3. **校准权衡**：4参数校准消除了跨时间窗口的单调性违规。Exponential AFT（对应Weibull k=1）的预期校准误差（ECE）极低（~1e-4），比Log-Normal低10倍，尽管两者的排名指标差异很小（<0.3%）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了一个在单一AFT家族内的原则性校准-排名权衡。建议在实际应用中，对于需要概率输出的场景采用Exponential AFT以实现高精度校准，而对于纯排名任务则采用Log-Normal以获得最佳排序性能。这一方法不仅简化了模型架构，还提高了效率和准确性。

Abstract: Repurchase recommenders in e-commerce are commonly framed as a binary question asking "will this customer buy this item within W days", a formulation that requires a separately trained model for every horizon of interest. We replace this stack with survival models that predict time-to-repurchase directly, and evaluate them on millions of customers from a major grocery e-commerce platform across more than thirty ablation configurations. Our study makes three contributions. First, an empirical hazard analysis reveals a slightly decreasing marginal hazard (k ~ 0.9), differing from the common intuition that grocery items become more likely to be repurchased the longer since the last purchase (increasing hazard, k > 1). Log-Normal achieves the best marginal fit (R^2 = 0.998) and the best ranking, despite Weibull providing the best conditional residual fit, revealing an apparent discrepancy we analyze in detail. Second, a single Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) model replaces three per-horizon binary classifiers, matching or exceeding each at its own horizon while using roughly 3x fewer total trees. Feature importance reshuffles under the survival objective: channel-cadence and recency signals rise while aggregate frequency counts fall. Third, a 4-parameter parametric calibration maps raw survival CDFs to per-horizon probabilities with zero cross-horizon monotonicity violations. Calibration quality varies by an order of magnitude across the AFT family: Exponential AFT (Weibull k=1) achieves expected calibration error (ECE) ~1e-4, roughly 10x lower than Log-Normal, while ranking metrics agree within 0.3% relative. We adopt Exponential AFT for probability-consuming surfaces and Log-Normal for pure ranking, exposing a principled calibration-ranking trade-off within a single AFT family.

</details>


### [218] [RetailAgent: Structured Adverse Timing in Self-Conditioned Multimodal LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28399)
*Yupeng Zhang,Liuyuan Jiang,Hongyi Huang,Bingheng Li,Lisha Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过RetailAgent框架发现，大型语言模型（LLM）在模拟金融交易时表现出可预测的负向择时结构。其买卖决策与后续回报之间存在系统性偏差，且这种偏差源于行动序列与回报的对齐及记忆机制的影响，揭示了LLM作为市场参与者可能存在的可被利用的行为信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融市场中，系统性地对价格变动做出反应的顺序策略可能会变得可预测。研究旨在探讨大型语言模型（LLM）代理是否在投资决策中表现出此类方向性结构，从而评估其作为市场参与者的可预测性及潜在风险。

Method: 构建了名为RetailAgent的实验框架，让LLM观察匿名化的日内股票价格历史和允许的状态信息，并在随后的区间回报揭示前重复选择“做多”（持有股票）或“平仓”（空仓）。通过移除整体做多决策比例，比较同一只股票日内路径上做多和平仓期间的回报，采用暴露匹配度量来评估择时能力。此外，还进行了动作序列洗牌实验和引入自撰记忆的实验以分析行为机制。

Result: 1. 存在持续的负向择时现象：在不同模态、时间跨度、状态和模型家族中均观察到LLM决策的负向择时效应。2. 对齐驱动效应：打乱保存的动作序列显著减弱了该效应，表明行动与后续回报的对齐是导致负分的主要原因。3. 记忆增强持续性：将自撰记忆纳入决策过程进一步增加了政策的持久性；在使用两种行动的交易日中，择时变得更负。

Conclusion: LLM的金融顺序决策中存在稳定且可恢复的方向性结构。这为研究其他市场参与者如何应对可预测政策提供了行为信号，同时也提示LLM在金融应用中的策略可能存在系统性缺陷或被套利风险。

Abstract: In financial markets, a sequential policy that reacts systematically to price movements may become predictable to other market participants. This paper studies whether large language model (LLM) agents exhibit such directional structure through RetailAgent, an experimental framework in which an LLM observes anonymized intraday equity price histories and permitted state, then repeatedly chooses long (hold the stock) or flat (stay out) before the subsequent interval return is revealed. We compare returns during long and flat intervals along the same stock's intraday path after removing the overall fraction of long decisions. This exposure-matched measure reveals persistent negative timing across modality, horizon, state, and model family. Shuffling saved action sequences substantially attenuates the effect, showing that alignment between actions and subsequent returns drives the negative score. Feeding self-authored memories into decisions further increases policy persistence, while timing becomes more negative among stock-days on which the agent uses both actions. These results reveal stable, recoverable directional structure in sequential LLM financial decisions and a behavioral signal for studying how another participant could respond to a predictable policy.

</details>


### [219] [VERA-8B: Evidence-Grounded Audit Risk Reasoning from SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28402)
*Menghan Liu,Elynn Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VERA-8B，一种端到端的审计推理系统，通过统一SFT和GRPO解决金融语言模型在审计中证据支撑不足的问题，并引入拒绝机制和AuditBridge工具以实现可审计、准备就绪的审计输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准金融语言模型优先考虑流畅性而非证据，导致答案虽然合理但模糊，存在“接地差距”（grounding gap），无法满足审计工作对合理证据支持的严格要求。

Method: 1. 构建VERA-8B模型，首次将监督微调（SFT）和基于梯度的策略优化（GRPO）统一用于证据驱动的审计推理；2. 引入拒绝机制（abstention）和不确定性资格认证（uncertainty qualification），以推迟处理不确定或缺乏证据的案例；3. 设计AuditBridge组件，将原始文件转化为验证记录，最终生成供审查者使用的报告。

Result: 该模型在性能上超越了所有评估的基线模型，能够识别执法行动发生前的审计风险，并产生可审计且适合实际审计工作的输出。

Conclusion: VERA-8B及其配套组件成功弥合了金融计算与审计实践之间的差距，提供了具备广泛通用性、证据 grounded 且符合审计标准的解决方案。

Abstract: Across audit applications, judgments must be supported by reasonable evidence. However, standard financial language models prioritize fluency over evidence. They are built for general financial reasoning and may produce plausible but ambiguous answers, creating a grounding gap that makes them unsuitable for audit work. We address this gap with VERA-8B, a new end-to-end audit reasoning system that identifies audit risks before enforcement actions occur. Constructing such a model raises several challenges, as no prior machine learning work targets pre-enforcement audit prediction. To our knowledge, we are the first to unify SFT and GRPO for evidence-grounded audit reasoning under one evidence standard, achieving performance that surpasses all evaluated baselines. Because auditing cannot tolerate unsupported claims, we introduce abstention and uncertainty qualification to defer uncertain or evidence-incomplete cases. Finally, we design an AuditBridge to ground model reasoning for practical audit work. It transforms raw filings into verified records and then into reviewer-ready reports, bridging finance and computation with broad generality. Together, these components produce auditable, review-ready outputs suitable for practical audit work.

</details>


### [220] [Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards: Symbolic Backpropagation for Post-Training LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28421)
*Vishvesh Bhat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PLVR方法，通过符号反向传播在模型外部构建可验证推理程序，以替代传统的权重更新微调，显著提升推理性能并降低新任务适应成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督微调和强化学习将推理能力固化在模型权重中，导致其不可检查、不可逐步验证且无法迁移至其他模型。对于中间步骤可验证的任务，作者主张将推理逻辑置于基础模型权重之外，作为由确定性和神经原语组成的显式程序。

Method: 引入PLVR（Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards）：利用符号反向传播机制，基于输入输出示例直接学习程序。该方法通过类型推断在原始签名上进行信用分配（类链式法则推导），而非估计；奖励基于每步合约判决密集分布于程序结构中，而非仅验证终端结果。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6和Tau2Bench上，30B基础模型使用PLVR比同等预算下的RL平均高出27.8分，比大一个数量级的前沿模型高出13.6分。仅需100个示例即可适应新任务，无需新的微调数据。消融实验表明，反向传递而非类型系统是性能优势的主要来源。

Conclusion: PLVR提供了一种高效、可验证的推理增强范式，通过释放符号反向传播库和一致性检查器，支持在其他原始库中应用该方法，证明了外部显式程序在特定推理任务上的优越性。

Abstract: Post training a language model to reason means updating its weights. Supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning both place the acquired capability inside the model where it cannot be inspected cannot be checked step by step and cannot be moved to another model. We argue that for tasks whose intermediate steps admit verification, reasoning is better placed outside the base models weights as an explicit program composed from deterministic and neural primitives. We introduce PLVR (Program Learning with Verifiable Rewards): a post training method that learns such programs directly from input-output examples. Its mechanism is symbolic backpropagation: each program layer carries a typed ontology a loss is computed at the output against ground truth and required input ontologies are propagated backward by type inference over primitive signatures: an analogue of the chain rule in which credit assignment is a derivation rather than an estimate. Where RLVR verifies a terminal outcome, PLVRs reward is a per step contract verdict dense over program structure. On LiveCodeBench v6 and Tau2Bench, 30B base models with PLVR outperform RL at matched budget by 27.8 points on average and frontier models an order of magnitude larger by 13.6 points. A single primitive library serves two benchmarks, so the marginal cost of a new task is 100 examples of program search and no new finetuning data. Replacing the loss guided search with uniform sampling over the same type admissible space at equal budget collapses the median program from 65.6 to 17.5, identifying the backward pass rather than the type system as the source of the advantage. We release the symbolic backpropagation library and a conformance checker so the method can be applied to primitive libraries other than our own.

</details>


### [221] [Prove2Me: An Open Collaborative Platform for Scaling Math Formalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28433)
*Shuze Chen,Kunal Marwaha,Xiaoyang Lu,Henry Yuen,Tianyi Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Prove2Me平台，利用AI编程代理降低形式化数学验证门槛，实现人机协作的大规模、可扩展的数学形式化项目。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Lean 4等大型形式化数学项目面临的高进入壁垒（需精通形式化验证及底层数学）和高时间成本问题，旨在通过AI编码代理降低技术门槛，实现互联网规模的数学协作。

Method: 开发开源协作平台Prove2Me，设计机制与专用工具包（harness），允许用户发起形式化“任务”，AI代理贡献证明并相互复用已有成果，支持大规模协作。

Result: 实现了基于自然语言提示的复杂证明生成，构建了可让AI代理构建彼此工作、自由复用结果的协作框架，将数学形式化转化为面向大众的众包努力。

Conclusion: 证明了结合人类与AI代理进行机器检查正确性的互联网规模数学协作是可行的，为形式化数学提供了可扩展、低门槛的新范式。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean 4 promise the paradigm of formally verified mathematics, but large-scale formalization projects have faced major barriers to entry, including the need for expertise in formal verification (as well as the underlying mathematics) and the significant time required for writing formal proofs. AI coding agents have dramatically reduced these barriers; human users can now use natural language to prompt agents to write complex proofs in Lean. This opens up the intriguing possibility of internet-scale mathematical collaboration involving both humans and AI agents, where correctness is machine-checked.
  To realize this possibility, we introduce Prove2Me (https://prove2.me), an open collaborative platform for formalizing mathematics. Users launch formalization "missions", to which AI agents contribute formal proofs toward completion. We designed mechanisms and a specialized harness in Prove2Me that enable large-scale collaboration so that agents can build on one another's work and freely reuse existing results. In doing so, Prove2Me aims to turn math formalization into a scalable, crowd-sourced effort open to anyone with an agent.

</details>


### [222] [Learning to Use Tools: Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28447)
*Minghui Xu,Zi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过整合计算器工具并结合监督微调（SFT）与强化学习（RL），显著提升了大语言模型在Countdown数学推理任务中的表现，其中Tool-DAPO方法将pass@1准确率从35.8%提升至66.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在处理需要可靠计算和验证的任务时，往往因算术错误导致推理失败；旨在通过外部工具集成解决这一问题，特别是针对Countdown任务中的数学推理能力。

Method: 首先分析推理失败原因并构建SFT数据集以教授工具使用模式；随后应用多种在线策略强化学习方法（RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, DAPO），利用自动可验证的最终答案奖励进行优化；同时构建了无数据重叠的1,024题新基准进行测试。

Result: 计算器工具集成一致性地改善了SFT和RL基线性能，在pass@k指标上获得约10个百分点的提升；Tool-DAPO表现最佳，pass@1从35.8%提升至66.0%；RL促使模型在仅有最终答案奖励的情况下也能更有效地使用工具。

Conclusion: 工具集成能有效减少算术和验证错误，而强化学习能增加正确推理轨迹的概率，两者结合可显著提升模型的数学推理可靠性。

Abstract: Current large language models (LLMs) increasingly benefit from external tool integration, especially for tasks requiring reliable computation and verification. Motivated by this, we study calculator tool calling for improving mathematical reasoning on the Countdown task. We first analyze reasoning failures and find that calculation errors account for a substantial portion of incorrect responses. We then construct supervised fine-tuning datasets to teach the model useful tool-use patterns and how to interpret returned outputs. Building on this tool-formatted policy, we apply several on-policy reinforcement learning methods, including RLOO, RLOO++, GRPO, and DAPO, using automatically verifiable final-answer rewards. To enable a more reliable evaluation, we construct a fresh 1,024-problem held-out Countdown benchmark with no exact overlap with the training data. Our results show that calculator tool integration consistently improves both SFT and RL baselines, yielding roughly 10 percentage-point gains across pass@k. Among the RL methods, Tool-DAPO achieves the strongest performance, improving pass@1 from 35.8% for Tool-SFT to 66.0%. Further analysis shows that RL encourages more effective tool use even when only final-answer rewards are provided. These findings suggest that tool integration reduces arithmetic and verification errors, while RL increases the probability of correct reasoning traces.

</details>


### [223] [COVER: Identifiable Evaluation of Coalition Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28475)
*Raghul Sugumar,Amrit Gopinath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出COVER评估契约，通过固定信息边界和下游栈来分离路由效应，揭示多智能体系统中选择潜力而非证明路由优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体系统变更团队时消息和答案变化导致的端到端准确率无法单独识别路由效应的难题。

Method: 引入覆盖完整性的评估契约，固定公共信息边界、下游栈G和法律团队族；使用源ID不相交分裂的控制表测试；在MuSiQue-12和HotpotQA-4数据集上进行实证分析，并通过ToolSandbox进行变体验证。

Result: 在预指定特权正控下遗憾值降低（如MuSiQue从0.532降至0.402）；在Llama执行中验证的路由遗憾改善0.190但原始答案增益不显著；工具沙盒变体验证显示声明族Oracle达到0.768完成度，而冻结路由器为0.637，未能满足预定标准。

Conclusion: COVER是一种可审计的测量方法论，揭示了选择空间（selection headroom），但未制造路由胜利的主张；绝对分数依赖于下游栈G，且未发现路由者与终结者之间的显著交互作用，不支持通用路由优越性。

Abstract: When a multi-agent system changes its team, it also changes the messages and final answer it produces, so an end-to-end accuracy gap does not by itself identify a routing effect. We introduce method, an evaluation contract that fixes a public information boundary, downstream stack G, and finite legal team family before outcomes are generated. Complete coverage identifies exact finite-benchmark oracle regret conditional on that stack. For any finite collection of frozen policies, executing the union of their distinct selected teams is the minimal assumption-free support for every pairwise policy contrast, though not for absolute oracle regret. Two controlled tables with source-ID-disjoint splits test the instrument. On MuSiQue-12, a pre-specified privileged positive control improves regret from 0.532 to 0.402; a later public-interface control reaches 0.424 versus 0.554 but is retrospective. On HotpotQA-4, a pre-specified public direct scorer improves regret from 0.313 to 0.110. In fixed-stack Llama execution, verified route regret improves by 0.190, while the raw-answer gain is 0.010 with an interval crossing zero. A five-family ToolSandbox variant-shift validation exhaustively evaluates 16 declared teams on 14 untouched task variants (224/224 valid rows): the declared-family oracle reaches 0.768 safe-evidence completion, while the prospectively frozen router gets 0.637 (regret 0.131), failing the predeclared 0.10 criterion. A later retrospective comparator reaches 0.655, matching all-workers with 4.57 versus 5.00 workers on average. Thus COVER exposes selection headroom without manufacturing a routing win. A crossed-stack diagnostic shows absolute scores depend on G but finds no detectable router-by-finalizer interaction. COVER is an auditable measurement methodology, not a claim of stack-invariant or universal agent-routing superiority.

</details>


### [224] [AcrossVAM1.0: Particle World Modeling for Text-Assisted Robot Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28491)
*Yafei Zhang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AcrossVAM1.0，一种轻量级文本辅助视频动作模型，通过将未来预测分解为中心物体的运动和密集外观，解决了机器人视频预测中运动推理与外观保留的纠缠问题。该模型利用冻结的SAM3-DLP编码器将上下文帧分解为语义粒子，通过时空Transformer对齐身份并推进状态，结合OpenCLIP指令嵌入和因果双流解码器生成未来帧。在VRS基准测试中，粒子动力学显著降低了轨迹误差并提高了PSNR/SSIM指标，但语言接地和外观交付仍是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单体像素模型在预测机器人视频时，往往混淆了精确的运动推理和高频外观保留这两个目标，且其进展常被强大的最后一帧基线所掩盖。因此，需要一种能够解耦这些目标的方法以提高预测性能。

Method: 提出了AcrossVAM1.0模型，包含以下核心组件：1) 冻结的SAM3-DLP编解码器，将四个上下文帧分解为机器人、机械臂、夹爪的语义粒子和背景潜在变量；2) 一个拥有0.28M参数的时空Transformer，用于对齐粒子身份、向前滚动状态，并通过FiLM机制受冻结的OpenCLIP指令嵌入调制；3) 因果双流解码器，结合粒子渲染的运动和仅从最后一帧编码的外观；4) 残差优化器和学习到的交付掩码，用于在不访问未来外观的情况下生成五个未来帧。

Result: 在由多样化真实机器人轨迹构成的VRS基准测试上：1) 粒子动力学使轨迹误差比持久性基线降低了21.0%；2) AcrossVAM1.0将未来帧的PSNR/SSIM从19.97/0.796提升至20.573/0.8004；3) 原始粒子生成的运动区域PSNR从11.89提升至13.23。然而，交付模型在LPIPS指标上尚未优于持久性基线，且正确与打乱的语言指令对轨迹误差的影响仅为2.8-3.1%。

Conclusion: 明确的粒子动力学是机器人视频预测中一种有前景的低维接口，但鲁棒的语言接地（language grounding）和外观交付（appearance delivery）仍是当前面临的主要开放挑战和限制因素。

Abstract: Predicting robot videos requires both precise motion reasoning and preservation of high-frequency appearance, yet monolithic pixel models entangle these objectives and often conceal their progress behind a strong last-frame baseline. We present AcrossVAM1.0, a lightweight, text-assisted video action model that factorizes future prediction into object-centric motion and dense appearance. A frozen SAM3-DLP codec decomposes four context frames into semantic particles for the robot, arm, and gripper, together with a background latent. A 0.28M-parameter spatio-temporal Transformer aligns particle identities, rolls their states forward, and is modulated by a frozen OpenCLIP instruction embedding through FiLM. A causal dual-stream decoder combines particle-rendered motion with appearance encoded exclusively from the last observed frame; a residual refiner and learned delivery mask produce five future frames without access to future appearance. On our VRS benchmark constructed from diverse real-robot trajectories, particle dynamics reduce trajectory error by 21.0\% over persistence. Across three delivery-mask seeds, AcrossVAM1.0 improves future-frame PSNR/SSIM from 19.97/0.796 to 20.573/0.8004, while raw particle generation improves motion-region PSNR from 11.89 to 13.23. The delivered model does not yet beat persistence in LPIPS, and correct-versus- shuffled language changes trajectory error by only 2.8--3.1%. We report these limitations alongside oracle, negative-control, multi-seed, and per-robot analyses. The results show that explicit particle dynamics are a promising low-dimensional interface for robot video prediction, while robust language grounding and appearance delivery remain the principal open challenges.

</details>


### [225] [Training Communication-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Language Models with Layer Re-Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28511)
*Simeng Sun,Roger Waleffe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CE-MoE架构，通过异构层模式解耦token混合与通道混合深度，集中专家容量至少量路由MoE层。在2B至31.5B参数规模下，该方法在保持模型性能的同时，显著降低了通信开销和训练成本（31.5B规模下减少33.3% GPU小时数），并提升了下游任务表现和推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用专家并行（expert parallelism）训练混合专家（MoE）语言模型时，所有的to-all token分发和合并集合操作会消耗端到端训练时间的很大一部分，导致通信效率低下。

Method: 提出通信高效的MoE模型（CE-MoE），采用异构层模式，将token-mixing（如注意力、Mamba-2）和channel-mixing（MoE层）的深度解耦。具体而言，CE-MoE将专家容量集中在少数几个路由的MoE层中，并通过增加额外的token-mixing层和密集FFN层来维持模型深度，而非像传统模型那样在每个token-mixing层后交错插入MoE层。

Result: 在2B到31.5B总参数的扩展阶梯上，在匹配总激活参数和激活参数总量的条件下，CE-MoE模型一致地降低了训练成本，同时验证损失和下游基准测试表现与全MoE基线持平。在31.5B规模下，CE-MoE减少了33.3%的GPU小时数，同时提高了平均下游得分和推理吞吐量。

Conclusion: CE-MoE通过重构网络层级结构优化了通信效率，证明了在大规模MoE训练中，减少MoE层频率并增加稠密层是平衡计算效率、通信开销和模型性能的有效策略。

Abstract: When training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models with expert parallelism, all-to-all token dispatch and combine collectives can consume a substantial fraction of end-to-end training time. In this work, we study communication-efficient MoE models (CE-MoE), in which we adopt a heterogeneous layer pattern that decouples token-mixing and channel-mixing depth. Compared to conventional models which interleave MoE layers after each token-mixing layer (e.g., attention, Mamba-2), CE-MoE models concentrate expert capacity in a select few routed MoE layers, while maintaining depth by adding additional token-mixing and dense-FFN layers. Across a scaling ladder from 2B to 31.5B total parameters, under matched total and activated parameters, CE-MoE models consistently reduce training cost while matching validation loss and downstream benchmarks with full-MoE baselines. At the 31.5B scale, CE-MoE uses 33.3\% fewer GPU-hours while improving average downstream score and inference throughput.

</details>


### [226] [InstructMesh: Selective Refinement of Generative 3D Models for Fabrication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28534)
*Faraz Faruqi,Ahmed Katary,Demircan Tas,Theresa Hradilak,Ning Zhang,Jiaji Li,Fabian Manhardt,Martin Nisser,Vrushank Phadnis,Ruofei Du,Federico Tombari,Megan Hofmann,Stefanie Mueller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InstructMesh 是一个交互式后生成优化工具，通过自然语言或滑块控制直接操作中间潜在表示，帮助用户修复生成式3D模型的几何缺陷，使其适用于制造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的生成式AI创建的3D模型虽然视觉逼真，但往往缺乏几何精度，存在影响后续制造的缺陷（如空洞、厚度不均）。现有工具要求用户具备专业建模技能，且缺乏针对这些特定缺陷的便捷修复手段。

Method: 提出 InstructMesh 系统，允许用户通过区域选择和针对性操作（如开启/密封空洞、调整局部厚度）来修复模型。该系统直接在中间潜在表示上操作，支持自然语言提示和滑块控制两种交互方式。研究首先分析了主流生成工具的制造相关故障模式，随后进行了两项用户研究以验证其有效性和设计偏好。

Result: 用户研究表明，初学者能够使用 InstructMesh 识别并执行与制造相关的修复操作。此外，研究发现用户倾向于混合界面设计，即结合滑块控制和自然语言输入。

Conclusion: InstructMesh 成功降低了非专家用户修复生成式3D模型几何缺陷的门槛，证明了在中间潜在表示上进行交互式几何修正的有效性，并为未来人机协作的3D编辑工具提供了设计启示。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI allow users to create 3D models from text or images. However, these models prioritize visual plausibility over geometric accuracy, often generating results with flaws that compromise their intended use post-fabrication. We present InstructMesh, an interactive post-generation refinement tool that enables selective repair of generative 3D models through region selection and targeted operations, such as opening or sealing voids, or adjusting local thickness. Users can invoke edit operations via natural language prompts or slider controls. By operating directly on the intermediate latent representation, InstructMesh allows users to apply robust geometric corrections without requiring expert modeling skills. To inform our design, we first analyze common fabrication-related failure modes in outputs from state-of-the-art generative tools. We then conduct two user studies, demonstrating that novices can identify and perform fabrication-relevant repairs on generative outputs using InstructMesh, and revealing user preference for hybrid interfaces that combine slider controls with natural language input.

</details>


### [227] [Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28553)
*Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Hao Cheng,Yi Gao,Bo Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现有代理系统（如spatiotemporal-composability calculus）中“插件即进程、单上下文共享”导致的故障域耦合问题，提出Logos框架。通过将插件解耦为独立进程并仅共享追加式日志（append-only transcript），实现了跨进程代理编排。实验表明，该架构显著提升了容错性：单一节点故障仅影响局部会话，而非中断所有共址会话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态代理组装形式（基于单一进程和共享上下文）将所有组件置于同一个物理故障域。这导致任何一次故障都会同时挂起所有组件，且进程死亡会中断其承载的所有会话，缺乏细粒度的故障隔离能力。

Method: 1. 理论分析：证明建模和计算法则并未将代理绑定到单一进程；利用语言模型推理的状态无关性（statelessness），将跨步骤状态移出模型，仅在状态空间上定义健全性不变量。
2. 构建Logos：基于推导出的四个引理，设计了一个类ROS的跨进程代理工具链（harness）。在该系统中，插件表现为独立进程，唯一的共享状态是一个追加式日志（append-only transcript）。

Result: 1. 在工具调用周期的四个边界处注入杀死信号后，80个会话均能恢复且无重复效应。
2. 与单进程参考配置的同故障对比显示：单进程架构下，一个故障会中断所有共存会话；而在对等进程（peer-process）架构下，一个故障仅终止于单个节点，实现了故障隔离。

Conclusion: 通过解耦代理插件至独立进程并利用追加式日志进行状态协调，可以有效打破物理故障域的耦合限制，显著提升多会话环境下的系统鲁棒性和故障隔离能力。

Abstract: Modern agent systems assemble capabilities at runtime, and this dynamic composition has recently received a complete formal treat ment in the spatiotemporal-composability calculus, in which a capability is a component carrying a tracked inverse, and agents are assembled as plugins. This plugin form is carried by a single process sharing one context, a carrier that places all components in one physical failure domain, a fault suspends every component at once, and process death interrupts every session the process hosts. This paper shows that neither the modeling nor the calculus binds an agent to one process, the statelessness of the language model keeps all cross-step state outside the model, and the soundness invariant is defined on the state space alone. These observations condense into four lemmas whose premises are the hypotheses of the calculus and the statelessness of language-model inference. On these lemmas this paper constructs Logos, a ROS-like cross process agent harness in which a plugin is a process and the only shared state is an append-only transcript. Eighty sessions resume with no repeated effect after kills placed at the four boundaries of the tool-call cycle, and a same-fault comparison with a single process reference configuration shows one fault interrupting every co-resident session while under the peer-process construction one fault ends at one node.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [LitCurate: A Configuration-Driven AI-Assisted Framework for Scientific Database Construction with an Application to Lower-Mantle Equation-of-State Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27629)
*Abin Shakya,Wilson Samuels,Dominica Wilson,Gioia A. Marchi,Israa Draz,Chenxing Luo,Renata M. Wentzcovitch*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LitCurate框架，利用大语言模型和可审计的工作流，从科学文献中自动构建结构化数据库，并以地幔状态方程数据库为例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大量科学实验和计算结果被锁定在出版物中、难以用于大规模分析或科学软件的问题；克服从海量论文中发现相关研究并提取具备科学语境数据的挑战。

Method: 开发LitCurate开源框架，集成文献发现、相关性筛选、全文处理和结构化信息提取四个阶段；使用大语言模型进行信息抽取，保留中间结果和溯源信息，支持研究人员检查和修订各个阶段，避免黑盒操作。

Result: 成功构建了包含205篇论文中1,334个条目的下地幔及相关高压矿物相的状态方程数据库；数据集将参数与矿物相、成分、公式、方法和约束条件关联，并标注数据来源（原始报告或引用报告）；提供可搜索的Web应用访问数据。

Conclusion: LitCurate提供了一种可重复的方法，通过连接科学文献与可追溯、机器可读的数据，将累积的文献转化为科学分析和计算建模的资源。

Abstract: The growing scientific literature contains decades of experimental and computational results that could support data-driven and physics-based modeling, yet much of this infor- mation remains locked in publications and is not readily usable for large-scale analysis or sci- entific software. Building structured databases from the literature is particularly challenging whenrelevantstudiesmustfirstbediscoveredamonglargecollectionsofpapersandreported quantities must be extracted with enough scientific context to remain usable. We present LitCurate, an open-source framework for building scientific databases from the literature using large language models within an auditable, stage-wise curation workflow. LitCurate integratesliteraturediscovery, relevancescreening, full-textprocessing, andstructuredinfor- mation extraction while retaining intermediate results and provenance, allowing researchers to inspect and revise individual stages rather than treating automated curation as a black- box process. We apply LitCurate to construct an equation-of-state database of lower-mantle and lower-mantle-relevant high-pressure mineral phases from experimental and theoretical studies, comprising 1,334 entries from 205 papers. The resulting dataset links reported equation-of-state parameters to mineral phases, compositions, equation formulations, meth- ods, and parameter constraints, and labels values as source-reported or citation-reported when provenance can be determined. The records are available through a searchable web application. By connecting scientific literature to traceable, machine-readable data, LitCu- rate provides a reusable approach for transforming accumulated literature into resources for scientific analysis and computational modeling.

</details>


### [229] [Beyond the Vacuum: Combinatorial Strategy Selection for Competitor-Aware Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27631)
*Vaibhav Sourirajan,Yao Zhang,Himanshu Kumar,Sahil Wadhwa,Mann Patel,Amirfarrokh Iranitalab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于竞争感知的生成式引擎优化（GEO）两阶段方法，通过组合结构贝叶斯优化搜索重写策略，并利用生成的偏好对微调语言模型以选择最优策略组合，在多个基准上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GEO方法孤立地选择重写策略，忽略了内容优化采用率增长带来的关键外部性：即随着优化普及，最佳重写策略会发生变化。

Method: 构建了一个两阶段流水线：(1) 使用组合结构的贝叶斯优化（BOCS）高效搜索重写策略空间；(2) 从BOCS黑盒观察中生成偏好对和接地推理轨迹，用于微调语言模型，使其能分析文档语料库并提出最优的重写策略组合。

Result: 在geo-bench及合成的竞争性数据集geo-bench_comp上，该方法在多项印象指标上超越了现有的智能体（agentic）和单启发式方法，达到最先进水平（SOTA），并证明其在多个分布外（OOD）数据集中具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将GEO形式化为竞争感知策略选择问题，提出的方法有效解决了策略动态变化问题，并在不同领域、查询和文档类型中证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) has emerged as a novel paradigm for transforming content to increase visibility in Large Language Model (LLM) responses. Traditional GEO methods, however, select rewriting strategies in isolation, ignoring a critical externality: as adoption of content optimization grows, optimal strategies for rewriting content change. We formalize GEO as a competitor-aware strategy selection problem and propose a two-phase pipeline to solve it: (1) We use Bayesian Optimization of Combinatorial Structures (BOCS) to efficiently search the space of rewriting strategies, (2) We generate preference pairs and grounded reasoning traces from the BOCS black-box observations to fine-tune a language model to analyze a document corpus and propose optimal rewriting strategy combinations. We achieve state-of-the-art performance across several impression metrics over existing agentic and single-heuristic methods on both geo-bench and our synthetically augmented competitive dataset geo-bench_comp. Our method also transfers to multiple out-of-distribution datasets, proving effective across domains, queries, and document types.

</details>


### [230] [A Versioned Unified Graph Index for Dynamic Timestamp-Aware Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27663)
*Jun Woo Chung,Weijie Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TiGER算法，通过构建统一的时间集成图结构，实现动态向量数据上任意时间范围的快速近似最近邻搜索，无需后处理过滤或合并子图，查询速度最高提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在动态向量数据集上进行时间感知近似最近邻搜索时，传统方法需要针对每个时间区间建立独立子图或进行复杂的后搜索过滤/合并操作，导致效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出TiGER（Time-Integrated Graph for Efficient Retrieval），利用基于集成版本连通性的索引结构，为所有向量构建并维护一个统一图。该结构允许直接在统一图上查询任意时间区间，自动规避无效向量，无需遍历或合并。

Result: 实证评估显示，该方法在保证精度的前提下，查询吞吐量（QPS）相比基于过滤或按时间分段子图的基线方法最高提升5倍。

Conclusion: TiGER方法能够支持实时推荐系统、日志分析等场景中随时间演变的动态数据的快速相似性搜索和高效时序分析。

Abstract: We present TiGER (Time-Integrated Graph for Efficient Retrieval), a novel approach for performing fast time-aware approximate nearest neighbor searches on dynamic vector datasets with flexibility over any possible time range. Our proposed algorithm builds and maintains a unified graph for all vectors by leveraging an index structure based on integrated versioned connectivity, allowing arbitrary time intervals to be queried directly on the unified graph without having to traverse invalid vectors. This forgoes the need for post-search filtering or merging, or separate graphs for each possible composite range. Empirical evaluations show that our method attains up to a 5x improvement in queries per second (QPS) without compromising accuracy over baselines based on filtering or per-time-segment sub-graphs. We believe that this method will enable efficient temporal analysis across evolving datasets in real-time recommendation systems, log analysis, and any scenario requiring fast similarity search over dynamic, time-segmented data.

</details>


### [231] [NormasTCU --- A Brazilian Portuguese IR Dataset and an Evaluation of LLM-as-a-Judge for Relevance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27746)
*Leandro Carísio Fernandes,Marcus Vinícius Borela de Castro,Leandro dos Santos Ribeiro,Leonardo Augusto da Silva Pacheco,Edans Flávius de Oliveira Sandes*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个巴西葡萄牙语法律信息检索数据集NormasTCU，并评估了LLM作为裁判在相关性判断中的可靠性。研究发现LLM存在正向评分偏差且与人工判断的一致性仅为中等水平，但在基于排名的指标（nDCG, MRR）上表现良好，而在基于精确率/召回率的指标上不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对葡萄牙语信息检索领域缺乏公开数据集、专业集合相关性评估成本高昂的问题，以及大语言模型在非英语专业领域可靠性不明的现状，旨在探索LLM在特定语言和专业领域相关性评估中的应用潜力与局限性。

Method: 构建包含14,469份法律文档、46个查询及3,048个人工判断的NormasTCU数据集；使用三种LLM模型结合两种提示技术对查询-文档对进行评分；比较由LLM生成和人工参考qrels推导出的15个IR系统的排名性能。

Result: LLM表现出明显的正向评分偏差（平均绝对误差0.46-0.66），与人工判断的一致性为fair到moderate（Cohen's kappa 0.32-0.53）。尽管存在偏差，LLM生成的排名在nDCG@10和MRR上与人工高度相似（Kendall's tau ≥ 0.90），但在P@10和R@10上可靠性较低。有趣的是，LLM排名有时比单个人工标注更贴近参考排名。

Conclusion: LLM可有效支持基于排名感知指标（如nDCG或MRR）的专业葡萄牙语语料库的可扩展相关性评估，但应避免在使用精确率或召回率等指标时依赖LLM生成的判断。

Abstract: Portuguese Information Retrieval (IR) lacks public datasets, and relevance assessment for specialized collections remains costly. While Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly support relevance assessment, their reliability in non-English specialized domains remains unclear. We introduce NormasTCU (https://huggingface.co/datasets/LeandroRibeiro/NormasTCU), a Brazilian Portuguese IR dataset with 14,469 legal documents, 46 queries, and 3,048 human judgments over 812 query-document pairs. Using NormasTCU, we evaluated LLM-as-a-judge for relevance assessment by prompting three models with two prompt techniques to grade these pairs. We then compared the rankings of 15 IR systems derived from LLM-generated and human reference qrels. LLMs consistently showed a positive scoring bias (mean absolute error: 0.46--0.66 on a 0-2 scale). Furthermore, pair-level agreement with human judgments achieved only fair to moderate levels, with Cohen's kappa ranging from 0.32 to 0.53. Despite this bias, LLM-generated judgments often yielded highly similar system rankings for nDCG@10 and MRR (observed Kendall's tau greater than or equal 0.90, although the bootstrap confidence intervals did not always remain above this threshold), but were less reliable for P@10 and R@10. Notably, LLM-based rankings were sometimes more strongly correlated with the reference ranking than individual human annotations were. As a practical implication, our results suggest that LLMs could effectively support scalable relevance assessment in specialized Portuguese corpora when evaluated using nDCG or MRR (rank-aware metrics), but they should be avoided when relying on precision or recall.

</details>


### [232] [LINE Conversation History Retrieval for Personal Memory RAG: Evaluating Search Representations and Hybrid Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27809)
*Akito Hattori*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究针对大语言模型个人记忆检索增强生成（RAG）进行了初步探索，通过LINE对话历史数据对比了不同文本表示与检索算法的效果。结果显示，结合摘要与原始文本的混合嵌入表示配合BM25和向量检索的线性组合（beta=0.45）性能最佳，Recall@5达0.697，但聚合类问题检索效果较差，且该研究仅为单用户、单标注者的探索性实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作为实现大语言模型个人记忆检索增强生成（RAG）的初始步骤，旨在探索如何有效从大量个人对话历史中检索相关信息，以支持后续的生成任务。

Method: 1. 数据预处理：将358,896条LINE消息分割为22,329个时间连贯的片段。
2. 构建三种搜索表示：原始文本（raw_text）、生成摘要（summary）、以及结合摘要与原始文本片段的混合嵌入文本（embedding_text）。
3. 评估方法：在100个验证问题上，比较BM25、稠密向量检索及线性混合检索三种策略，并测试了126种配置（6种配对方式×21种权重）。

Result: 1. 单一检索器中，embedding_text_bm25表现最好，Recall@5为0.584。
2. 最佳混合配置为embedding_text_bm25与embedding_text_vector结合（beta=0.45），Recall@5提升至0.697，MRR@5为0.595，nDCG@5为0.575。
3. 相比纯BM25提升显著（置信区间[0.048, 0.184]），但与另一混合方案（beta=0.50）无显著差异。
4. 聚合类问题（需跨多个时间和对话整合证据）的检索分数较低，表明扁平化片段检索在此类场景下存在局限。

Conclusion: 基于混合嵌入表示的线性混合检索在个人对话历史检索中表现出优于单一检索器的性能，证明了该方向的有效性。然而，当前结果受限于单用户、单标注者及固定问题集，未涉及最终答案生成及泛化能力评估，仍需进一步研究以解决跨上下文证据整合难题并验证通用性。

Abstract: As an initial step toward personal memory retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for large language models (LLMs), this study presents a retrieval-only case study over one user's LINE conversation history. We segmented 358,896 messages into 22,329 temporally coherent chunks and constructed three search representations: raw_text, a generated summary, and embedding_text, which combines a summary with a raw-text excerpt and other fixed text. We compared BM25, dense vector retrieval, and linear hybrid retrieval on 100 evaluation questions verified by a single annotator. Among individual retrievers, embedding_text_bm25 achieved the highest point estimate, with Recall@5 of 0.584. We then explored six retriever pairings and 21 weights, for 126 configurations on the same evaluation set. The selected combination of embedding_text_bm25 and embedding_text_vector at beta = 0.45 achieved Recall@5 = 0.697, MRR@5 = 0.595, and nDCG@5 = 0.575. Its Recall@5 exceeded that of embedding_text_bm25 by 0.113, with a question-level paired percentile-bootstrap 95% confidence interval of [0.048, 0.184]. This interval is conditional on fixing the configuration selected on the same 100 questions and does not account for uncertainty from configuration selection or weight search. The difference from a summary-based hybrid at beta = 0.50 was 0.050, with a 95% confidence interval of [-0.013, 0.115], so no clear difference could be established. The 17 aggregate questions also yielded lower point estimates than the other question types, suggesting that flat chunk-level retrieval struggles when evidence is distributed across multiple times and conversations. This evaluation is an exploratory single-user, single-annotator study conducted on the same question set used for configuration search; it does not evaluate final answer generation or generalization to unseen questions.

</details>


### [233] [Personalized and Multi-View Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27826)
*Jaehyung Lim,Wonbin Kweon,Woojoo Kim,Junyoung Kim,Dongha Kim,Hwanjo Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PMFRec，一种用于联邦冷启动推荐的个性化多视图表示方法，解决现有方法在固定物品池假设下的局限，通过个性化生成器、多视图编码器和知识融合机制提升推荐性能、公平性及隐私鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐（FedRec）大多假设物品池固定，忽略了实际场景中持续有新物品加入的“冷物品”设定。在双重约束下（服务器无法访问客户端交互数据，客户端无法访问服务器专有物品属性特征），先前的联邦冷启动推荐存在三大结构缺陷：缺乏个性化、强制异构语义进入单一嵌入空间导致组合性失败、以及因协同与属性表示显式对齐带来的训练和通信低效。

Method: 提出PMFRec（Personalized and Multi-view Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation）。该方法包含：1. 学习个性化表示生成器，从属性特征生成用户特定的物品表示；2. 引入具有物品自适应门控和正交目标的全球多视图编码器，以捕获互补语义视图并减少跨视图冗余；3. 将协同知识与属性知识融合为单一交换的物品表示，消除对客户端正则化的需求并降低通信开销。

Result: 在真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，PMFRec在冷物品推荐任务中一致优于强基线模型，同时进一步提升了用户级别的公平性、热场景适应性以及在局部差分隐私（LDP）下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PMFRec有效解决了联邦冷启动推荐中的个性化缺失、语义组合失效及通信效率低下问题，为动态物品环境下的隐私保护推荐提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: Federated recommendation (FedRec) enables personalized modeling without centralizing users' interaction histories, but most existing methods assume a fixed item pool and thus overlook the practical cold-item setting where new items continuously arrive. Under the dual-sided constraint, where the server cannot access clients' interactions while clients cannot access the server's proprietary item attribute features, prior federated cold-start recommendation approaches suffer from three structural limitations: a lack of personalization, compositionality failure caused by forcing heterogeneous semantics into a single embedding space, and training- and communication-inefficiency arising from explicit alignment between separate collaborative and attribute representations. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized and Multi-view Representation for Federated Cold-Start Recommendation (PMFRec). PMFRec learns a personalized representation generator to produce user-specific item representations from attribute features, and introduces a global multi-view encoder with item-adaptive gating and an orthogonality objective to capture complementary semantic views while reducing cross-view redundancy. In addition, PMFRec fuses collaborative and attribute knowledge into a single exchanged item representation, eliminating the need for an explicit client-side regularizer and reducing communication overhead. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that PMFRec consistently outperforms strong baselines in cold-item recommendation and further improves user-level fairness, warm-scenario adaptability, and robustness under Local Differential Privacy (LDP).

</details>


### [234] [ITER: Interaction-Aware Retrieval for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27912)
*Haodong Chen,Shuai Wang,Yu Yin,Shengyao Zhuang,Guido Zuccon,Teerapong Leelanupab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出iter，一种感知智能体交互的稠密检索器，通过整合主问题及历史子查询来增强表示，并利用轨迹相对学习信号进行训练，在InfoSeek-Eval和BrowseComp-Plus上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索器训练仅利用当前步骤的子查询和搜索结果作为信号，未能充分利用前序交互中积累的信息，导致信息利用率不足。

Method: 引入iter模型，将主问题和 preceding sub-queries（前置子查询）融入查询表示；采用基于智能体交互轨迹的相对学习信号进行训练；利用先前访问且有用的文档作为冗余负样本以提供最强的轨迹相对监督。

Result: 在三个模型家族的六个智能体骨干网络上，iter平均在InfoSeek-Eval上提升7.5%，在BrowseComp-Plus上提升13.5%，均优于LRAT；相比依赖外部LLM裁判信号的AgentIR，展现出更强的跨智能体鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 整合历史交互信息（主问题和前置子查询）并结合轨迹相对学习信号，能显著提升深度研究智能体的检索性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deep-research agents answer complex user questions through an iterative sequence of search steps, where the agent autonomously formulates sub-queries to retrieve the evidence needed at each stage. However, existing retriever training typically relies only on the sub-query and its corresponding search results at the current step as training signals, leaving the information accumulated from previous interactions largely underutilized.
  We introduce iter, an agent interaction-aware dense retriever trained using agent trajectory learning signals. iter represents each query by incorporating not only the current sub-query, but also the main question and preceding sub-queries, and is trained using trajectory-relative learning signals derived from the agent's interactions.
  Across six agent backbones from three model families, iter consistently outperforms the existing agent-trajectory-trained dense retriever, LRAT, achieving an average improvement of 7.5% on InfoSeek-Eval and 13.5% on BrowseComp-Plus. iter also demonstrates stronger cross-agent robustness than AgentIR, a deep-research retriever that relies on external LLM-judge signals and the agent's pre-search reasoning. Ablations further show that the main question and previous sub-queries provide the most robust query representation, while previously visited and useful documents, used as redundancy negatives in subsequent searches, provide the strongest trajectory-relative supervision. Code is available at https://github.com/ielab/ITER.

</details>


### [235] [Information-Guided Selective Modality-Interest Alignment for Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27950)
*Wenze Ma,Chenyu Sun,Yanmin Zhu,Qiwen Gu,Xuhao Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AMUR框架，通过信息引导的模态-兴趣选择性对齐，解决多模态推荐中噪声干扰问题，提升推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接融合所有模态信息可能引入噪声或弱相关信号，现有方法在模态选择上缺乏明确目标且对齐过程隐式或启发式。

Method: 基于信息论视角，先细化模态图结构以贴近用户行为，再跨模态选择性对齐共享的兴趣相关语义，保留互补信息。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上的实验表明，AMUR优于具有竞争力的基线模型，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: AMUR能有效增强与用户兴趣相关的模态信息并减少无关信号影响，为多模态推荐提供了更清晰的模态选择与对齐机制。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation (MMRec) aims to enhance recommendation performance by leveraging rich item content from multiple modalities. However, directly incorporating all modality information does not necessarily lead to better preference modeling, since user interests are often more related to a subset of modality signals, while other signals may be weakly aligned with user preferences or even introduce noise. Although recent MMRec methods improve modality utilization through invariant learning, attention mechanisms, graph refinement, or contrastive learning, their alignment processes are often implicit or heuristic and lack a clear objective for selecting modality signals that better match user interests.
  In this paper, we propose AMUR, an information-guided selective modality-interest alignment framework for multimodal recommendation. Inspired by an information-theoretic view, AMUR aims to enhance modality information that is more related to user interests while reducing the influence of less aligned signals. Specifically, AMUR first refines modality graph structures towards user behavior, and then selectively aligns shared interest-related semantics across modalities. This enables AMUR to improve modality-interest alignment while preserving useful modality-specific complementary information. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AMUR over competitive baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/Wenze1/AMUR.

</details>


### [236] [HubMixer: Progressive Latent Hub Mixing for Parameter-Efficient Feature Interaction in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27991)
*Jie Zhou,Zixian Gong,Wenhao Li,Chang Liu,Enzhao Shen,Bo Liu,Xu Guo,Fei Pan,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出HubMixer，一种通过可学习的潜在枢纽（latent hubs）组织特征交互的轻量级架构，解决推荐系统中异构特征直接混合导致的参数低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐中，用户、物品、行为等异构特征处于不同语义空间且交互稀疏。直接在原始空间混合所有token会导致参数效率低下，因为模型需隐式学习特征组的交互路由。

Method: 提出HubMixer架构，采用“诱导-交互-读取”范式：1) 枢纽诱导：通过交叉注意力将异构token压缩为紧凑的潜在枢纽；2) 枢纽交互：在干净的潜在空间中执行高阶交互；3) 条件读取：让原始token选择性读取交互后的枢纽，保留字段身份并注入全局语义。

Result: 离线实验显示HubMixer优于当前最先进（SOTA）模型。在线A/B测试中，快手短视频招聘业务简历提交转化率显著提升5.48%，且已全量上线生产环境。

Conclusion: HubMixer通过引入潜在枢纽机制，实现了高效且参数节约的特征交互，在工业级推荐场景中取得了显著的性能提升和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Learning effective feature interactions is central to industrial recommendation and advertising ranking systems. Recent token-mixing architectures simplify self-attention with lightweight mixing operators, improving hardware efficiency and enabling large-scale deployment. However, recommendation tokens are fundamentally heterogeneous: user profiles, item attributes, behavioral sequences, context features, statistical signals, and business-side features live in different semantic spaces and interact in sparse, sample-specific patterns. Directly mixing all tokens in the raw heterogeneous token space may therefore be parameter-inefficient, as the model must implicitly discover which feature groups should interact and how such interactions should be routed. In the paper, we propose HubMixer, a parameter-efficient latent hub mixing architecture for feature interaction in recommendation. Instead of directly mixing raw feature tokens, HubMixer introduces a small set of learnable latent hubs to organize feature interactions through an `induction--interaction--readout` paradigm. First, hub induction summarizes heterogeneous tokens into compact latent hubs, where latent hubs query input tokens through cross-attention. Second, hub interaction performs high-order interaction in the cleaner latent hub space. Third, token-conditioned readout lets each original token selectively read from the interacted hubs, injecting global interaction semantics while preserving token-level field identity. Extensive offline experiments on industrial recommendation tasks show that HubMixer outperforms the SOTA models. Online A/B testing in the Kuaishou short-video recruitment business further shows a statistically significant 5.48% improvement in resume submission conversion rate, and HubMixer has been fully deployed in production.

</details>


### [237] [Every Article Deserves a Video: Contextual Video Matching for Digital Publishers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28359)
*Arnaud Corone,Brice Pierre de la Briere,Gladys Roch,Samuel Leonardo Gracio,Yassine Bouher,Parvati Chauchaix*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Dailymotion开发的“上下文视频匹配”系统，利用大语言模型和文本嵌入技术，自动将相关视频与文本文章匹配，解决大规模内容管理难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字出版商面临管理海量内容目录的挑战，手动在庞大视频库中筛选相关视频不切实际，亟需自动化方案以提升变现能力和用户留存率。

Method: 提出“上下文视频匹配”系统，通过结合大语言模型（LLMs）和文本嵌入技术，实现文本-heavy网页/文章与相关视频的自动匹配，并详细阐述了其架构、评估及在生产环境中的部署。

Result: 该系统已在Dailymotion生产环境中上线并被数百家出版商采用，显著提升了用户参与度，并通过提供高度相关的视频内容丰富了用户体验。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的自动化视频匹配方案是解决大规模出版商内容整合问题的有效途径，具有显著的实用价值和商业潜力。

Abstract: As digital publishers face the challenge of managing massive content catalogs, the ability to effectively embed relevant video within text-based articles has become critical for both monetization and user retention. However, manual selection is impractical for large scale publishers, especially when navigating their own extensive video libraries or the entire global Dailymotion catalog. In this paper, we present the "Contextual Video Matching" system, a solution that automatically matches relevant videos with text-heavy web pages and articles. By leveraging Large Language Models (LLMs) and textual embeddings, we provide a scalable solution for publishers to efficiently combine video content with their articles. We discuss in detail the motivations, architecture, evaluations, and deployment of this system within Dailymotion's production environment. Since its launch, the system has been adopted by hundreds of publishers, significantly increasing user engagement and enriching user experiences with highly relevant video content.

</details>


### [238] [SG-UMP: Sequence-Guided Universal Multimodal Prioritization Calculation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28503)
*Xinyi Zhang,Yutong Li,Peijie Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SG-UMP框架，通过模块组合器和路由器动态适应用户偏好和数据集模态偏差，提升多模态序列推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以捕捉用户级偏好异质性和数据集级模态偏差，限制了跨用户和数据集的适应性。

Method: 构建SG-UMP插件，包含用于灵活处理的模块组合器（Module Combiner）和用于动态排序的模块路由器（Module Router）。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上实验表明，SG-UMP在不同骨干网络和模态设置下均能一致提升推荐性能。

Conclusion: 该插件式框架有效解决了多模态信息处理中的适应性问题，具有通用性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: Multimodal sequential recommendation (MSR) improves recommendation by incorporating heterogeneous information such as text, images, and user interactions. However, existing MSR methods often fail to capture user-level preference heterogeneity and dataset-level modality bias, limiting their adaptability across users and datasets. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{S}equence-\textbf{G}uided \textbf{U}niversal \textbf{M}ultimodal \textbf{P}rioritization Calculation Framework (\textbf{SG-UMP}), a plug-and-play plugin for enhancing multimodal information processing in MSR. SG-UMP includes a Module Combiner for flexible multimodal processing and a Module Router for dynamic module ordering, enabling adaptation to both user preferences and dataset characteristics. Experiments on four real-world datasets show that SG-UMP consistently improves recommendation performance across different backbones and multimodal settings. The code is available at https://github.com/esemsc-xz524/SG-UMP .

</details>


### [239] [QUEST: A Query and Extraction System for Topics in Asylum Law Application Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28555)
*Maria Vlachou,Anna Murphy Høgenhaug,Mohammad N. S. Jahromi,Galadrielle Humblot-Renaux,Thomas Gammeltoft-Hansen,Thomas B. Moeslund,Desmond Elliott*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出QUEST系统，用于从丹麦庇护上诉材料中提取和识别与可信度评估相关的因素，通过信息检索框架结合合成查询生成、主题提取和相关性评估，并引入新的领域特定评估指标以衡量系统在可信度因子上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 庇护申请的法律文件复杂且异构，当申请被拒时，需判断原决定是否基于对申请人信息的可信度评估，因此需要从上诉材料中有效提取与可信度相关的因素以辅助决策。

Method: 构建QUEST（Query and Extraction System for Topics）系统，将问题建模为信息检索任务，结合合成查询生成、主题提取和相关性评估技术；同时提出区别于传统相关性的领域特定评估指标，专门针对可信度因子进行性能评估。

Result: 通过实验发现，使用基于可信度的相关性评估在估算性能时面临更大挑战，表明该任务具有高度复杂性，自动方法在不同类型可信度指标上的表现存在差异。

Conclusion: 尽管自动方法能返回部分答案，但基于可信度的评估揭示了任务的难度，强调了在处理法律文本中可信度因素提取时的挑战性，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Legal decisions on asylum applications consist of long, complex, and heterogeneous documents, covering narrative applicant interviews, original decisions, and additional supporting materials. If an application is rejected, a critical question in processing an appeal is whether the credibility of the information in the original application was a factor that determined the original decision. In this paper, we present the QUEST system (Query and Extraction System for Topics) to extract and identify factors relating to credibility assessments in two datasets of Danish asylum application appeals. QUEST frames this problem as an information retrieval task, combining synthetic query generation, topic extraction, and relevance assessment to identify information related to credibility indicators in appeals board application materials. In addition to standard retrieval evaluation metrics, we propose a new type of domain-specific assessments distinct from the traditional relevance to evaluate the performance of the tested systems with respect to credibility factors. In this way, we obtain insights about how well automatic methods can return answers for different types of indicators appearing in asylum appeals. Our results indicate that there is an increased challenge when estimating performance using credibility-based relevance assessments, thus pointing to the difficulty of the task.

</details>


### [240] [PULSAR: Pooled Unified Late-Interaction Search and Retrieval for Enterprise Visual Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28572)
*Benjamin Constable,Anup Roy,Vishal Sharma,Rishabh Upadhyay,Robin Mills,Aidan Millar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PULSAR 是 Mubadala 部署的生产级视觉优先检索系统，通过 ColPali 骨干网络和两阶段晚期交互索引，显著降低延迟与成本，并提升事实召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构投资者需处理频繁更新的视觉密集型文档（如路演材料），传统 OCR 结合图表文本化的方案成本高、刷新困难且易丢失图表细节。

Method: 采用冻结的 ColPali 风格骨干网络对页面图像进行索引；使用池化两阶段晚期交互索引机制：先用紧凑的页面摘要进行初步检索，再通过更精细的池化表示进行 MaxSim 重排序。

Result: 在 ViDoRe V3 上，相比非池化配置，中位数向量搜索延迟降低 15.1 倍（损失 <0.01）；生产环境中延迟为 156ms，并发负载下 QPS 提高约 88 倍；每页摄入成本降低约 20 倍；Top K 答案事实召回率翻倍。已服务 7.8 万份文档和 240 万页。

Conclusion: PULSAR 证明了视觉优先检索在大规模金融尽职调查场景中的高效性与经济性，优于传统的 OCR+ 文本化基线。

Abstract: Institutional investors search visually dense pitch decks, board packs, and diligence materials that change hourly near deal closing. OCR followed by figure verbalisation is costly to refresh at this scale and can lose chart detail. We present PULSAR, a production vision-first retrieval system deployed at Mubadala Investment Company. PULSAR indexes page images with a frozen ColPali-style backbone and uses a pooled two-stage late-interaction index: compact page summaries support initial retrieval, followed by exact MaxSim rescoring over a finer pooled representation. On ViDoRe V3, this design reduces median vector-search latency by 15.1 times against an unpooled configuration with less than 0.01 absolute NDCG@10 and Recall@10 loss; production median vector-search latency is 156 ms. Under concurrent load, the pooled index sustains approximately 88 times higher QPS than an unpooled index. The event-driven ingestion path is estimated to be approximately 20 times cheaper per page than the OCR+verbalisation baseline it replaced. Since March 2026, PULSAR has served 78 thousand documents and approximately 2.4 million pages across more than 3,000 deals. At the production top K, it more than doubles answer-fact recall over the OCR+verbalisation baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Real-time SQL Plan Management in Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27758)
*Sunil Chakkappen,Mohamed Ziauddin,Hong Su,Shreya Kunjibettu,Nigel Bayliss*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出实时SQL计划管理（Real-Time SPM），通过在用户查询执行期间进行前台验证，解决传统后台验证导致的回归检测延迟问题，实现Oracle 26ai中执行计划的快速采纳与回归即时预防。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统存储大纲限制性能改进，而Oracle 11g的SQL计划管理（SPM）虽允许计划演进，但依赖后台验证过程，导致回归检测和恢复延迟。在自主云数据库系统中，自动操作引发的计划变更可能导致性能回归，且受限的后台资源难以及时应对，亟需一种能即时检测和修复问题的机制。

Method: 引入实时SPM（Real-Time SPM），利用运行时会话上下文在用户查询执行期间对新的执行计划进行前台验证。该技术挑战包括可靠地比较先前计划与新计划的性能，并对比了其与传统的后台计划演进机制的差异。

Result: 实现了新优越计划的快速采纳，同时能够 promptly（迅速）检测和防止执行计划回归，解决了云环境中后台资源不足导致的监控滞后问题。

Conclusion: 实时SPM通过前台验证机制有效平衡了计划稳定性与性能优化需求，为自主云数据库提供了更可靠的执行计划管理能力。

Abstract: Consistent query performance is essential for mission critical database applications, yet SQL execution plans can change due to factors such as database upgrades, DML changes, new indexes, etc. While plan stability mechanisms such as stored outlines prevent regressions by freezing execution plans, they also inhibit performance improvements by disallowing plan evolution. We introduced SQL Plan Management (SPM) in Oracle 11g to address this trade-off by maintaining a set of accepted execution plans and allowing plan evolution only when new plans demonstrably outperform existing baselines. However, prior implementations of SPM primarily rely on background performance verification processes, delaying regression detection and recovery. This issue is amplified in autonomous cloud database systems, where several automatic actions that could cause plan change driven regressions are performed with limited customer control. Timely detection and remediation is paramount, but the constrained background resources on cloud may not keep pace. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Real-Time SPM in Oracle 26ai, a novel extension of SPM that performs foreground verification of new execution plans during user query execution. Real-Time SPM leverages runtime session context to immediately validate plan changes, enabling rapid adoption of superior plans while promptly detecting and preventing regressions. This paper presents the architecture and design of Real-Time SPM - including technical challenges like reliably comparing performance of previous plans - and contrasts it with traditional background plan evolution.

</details>


### [242] [ANCHOR: A Vision for Secure Persistent Key-Value Stores in Disaggregated Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27819)
*Viraj Thakkar,Dongha Kim,Hokeun Kim,Zhichao Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对分离式持久键值存储（PKVS）中因计算、内存和存储分离导致的信任边界扩大问题，提出ANCHOR架构。该架构通过语义感知的双路径设计（持久路径加密防回滚、易失路径基于TEE的可验证缓存），实现端到端的数据完整性与新鲜度保障，同时优化性能以维持系统的弹性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着PKVS在分离式环境中的广泛部署，数据需跨多个主机传输、缓存和重写，导致信任边界重新划定。这种变化使得数据更容易受到网络攻击者和基础设施内部敌手的威胁，亟需解决跨主机操作中的数据完整性和新鲜性问题。

Method: 提出一种名为ANCHOR的两部分语义感知架构：1) 持久路径：对PKVS持久文件进行加密和认证，并通过清单版本控制防止回滚攻击；2) 易失路径：将缓存、索引和过滤器视为不可信提示，除非附带由TEE驻留策略强制执行的可验证来源证明。此外，还探讨了适合Enclave的批量处理和异步I/O技术，以分摊验证开销。

Result: 构建了一个能够保障分离式PKVS端到端完整性和新鲜度的愿景框架。通过结合加密认证、版本控制和可信执行环境（TEE）策略，有效抵御了网络攻击和内部威胁，同时利用批量处理和异步I/O机制平衡了安全性与系统性能及弹性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: ANCHOR为分离式持久键值存储提供了全面的安全保障方案，证明了在保持高性能和弹性的前提下，可以实现跨主机数据流的强完整性与新鲜度保证，为未来分布式存储系统的安全设计提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Persistent key-value stores (PKVS) are increasingly deployed in disaggregated settings that split compute, memory, and storage across separate server pools. This shift redraws the trust boundary: data that would remain within a single machine is now transported, cached, and rewritten across multiple hosts, expanding exposure to both network attackers and intra-infrastructure adversaries.
  This paper presents ANCHOR, a vision for end-to-end integrity and freshness in disaggregated PKVS. ANCHOR proposes a two-part semantics-aware architecture: 1) Persistence path: ANCHOR outlines encrypting and authenticating PKVS persistent files and preventing rollback with manifest versioning. 2) Volatile path: ANCHOR treats caches, indexes, and filters as untrusted hints unless accompanied by verifiable provenance, enforced by a TEE-resident policy. Finally, we outline key invariants and discuss enclave-friendly batching and asynchronous I/O to amortize verification without undermining disaggregation's performance and elasticity benefits.

</details>


### [243] [DBRepro: Automated Database Synthesis via a Hybrid Constraint-Solving Approach for Reproducing Slow Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27822)
*Zhaoyang Zhang,Shuang Liu,Dengfeng Xu,Wei Lu,Jianquan Leng,Sheng Du,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: DBRepro 是一个自动化端到端框架，通过将数据库生成建模为约束分布合成问题，解决慢查询离线诊断难题。它在保持全局统计分布的同时精确满足局部基数约束，显著优于现有数据驱动和工作负载感知基线，并在真实世界大数据集上验证了高保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢查询导致数据库性能瓶颈，在线诊断加剧资源竞争且受限于数据隐私法规无法复制生产数据。现有方法无法同时满足保留全局统计分布和强制执行精确局部基数这两个高保真复现需求。

Method: 提出 DBRepro 框架，将数据库生成形式化为约束分布合成问题。该方法从轻量级列统计信息初始化全局分布，从目标查询中提取执行约束，并逐步调整分布以满足约束同时保持全局分布特性。

Result: 在 TPC-H 和 SSB 基准测试中，相比数据驱动基线，基数误差降低高达 20.3%；相比工作负载感知基线，一致执行计划复现率提高 15%，延迟比例误差降低 21.5%。在 KingbaseES 管理的近 TB 级真实数据集上，成功高保真复现复杂慢查询的执行性能。

Conclusion: DBRepro 通过结合全局统计与局部约束的分布合成策略，有效实现了非侵入式、高保真的数据库代理生成，为慢查询的离线根因分析提供了可靠且隐私安全的解决方案。

Abstract: Slow queries frequently cause severe performance bottlenecks in database management systems. Diagnosing their root causes online risks exacerbating resource contention, while data privacy regulations often prohibit copying production data to test environments. Synthesizing a proxy database from non-intrusive metadata that induces the query optimizer to generate the same physical execution plans is therefore critical for offline diagnosis. High-fidelity reproduction requires preserving global statistical distributions while enforcing exact local cardinalities. Existing data-driven and workload-aware approaches cannot satisfy both requirements simultaneously.
  We present DBRepro, an automated end-to-end framework that formulates database generation as a constrained distribution synthesis problem. DBRepro initializes a global distribution from lightweight column statistics, extracts execution constraints from target queries, and progressively adjusts the distribution to satisfy these constraints while preserving the global distribution. Experiments on TPC-H and SSB show that DBRepro reduces cardinality error by up to 20.3% over a data-driven baseline while maintaining identical plan consistency. Compared with a workload-aware baseline, it reproduces 15% more consistent execution plans and reduces latency proportion error by 21.5%. We further validate DBRepro on a nearly 1 TB real-world dataset managed by KingbaseES, where it reproduces the execution performance of complex slow queries with high fidelity.

</details>


### [244] [VeriTS: Verifiable Model-Enhanced Time-Series Queries on Blockchain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28318)
*Zhongming Yao,Jun Pang,Chenxu Wang,Qian Ma,Peiyuan Guan,Shiliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出VeriTS，首个可验证区块链时间序列查询框架，通过认证聚合区间树实现高效、可验证的范围和聚合查询，无需修改链上存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链系统仅支持基本块/交易查找，无法处理时间序列查询（如时间范围检索、窗口聚合）；离线查询虽增强表达能力但不可信，需结果可验证。

Method: 构建离线索引层，使用认证聚合区间树表示数据流；通过最小覆盖集验证完整性，通过聚合折叠验证正确性；扩展至近似路径模型，重新定义有界误差下的完整性和正确性；矿工验证模型残差而非重放计算。

Result: 在窗口聚合场景下，验证效率比逐记录证明提升两个数量级以上；范围查询证明大小最大缩减14.5倍；即使恶意编码器也无法损害结果正确性。

Conclusion: VeriTS有效解决了区块链时间序列查询的可验证性与效率问题，为链上数据的时间序列分析提供了实用化方案。

Abstract: Blockchain data is temporal. Every transaction carries a timestamp and the chain imposes a total order, so on-chain data forms per-source time-series streams. However, existing systems support only basic lookups on blocks and transactions, and cannot answer time-series queries such as time-range retrieval and windowed aggregation. Offloading queries off-chain restores expressiveness, but the off-chain query layer is untrusted, so results must be verifiable. To this end, we propose VeriTS, the first verifiable time-series query framework for blockchain systems. It supports efficient range and aggregation queries without altering blockchain storage structures. VeriTS maintains an off-chain query layer that represents each stream through an authenticated aggregate interval tree. The tree serves as the query index and as the authenticated data structure at once, so a windowed aggregate is answered by folding a logarithmic number of node aggregates. VeriTS verifies completeness through a minimum covering set and soundness through aggregate folding. It extends both guarantees to an approximate path over model segments, redefining completeness and soundness under bounded error. Miners validate a model's residual rather than replay its computation, so even an adversarial encoder can inflate proof size and answer width but never correctness. Experiments offer evidence that on windowed aggregation, VeriTS improves verification efficiency by more than two orders of magnitude over per-record proofs. Range-query proofs shrink by up to 14.5x.

</details>


### [245] [No Silver Bullet: Boosting GaussDB Performance on the 30TB TPC-H Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28352)
*Tim Zeyl,Jason Lam,Shu Lin,Reza Pournaghi,Qi Cheng,Calvin Wong,Kaixiang Du,Yuliang He,Yang Sun,Weicheng Wang,Paul Lee,Chen Ruo,Yang Xinyi,Li Qunan,Wang Junjie,Hu Dongxing,Chong Chen,Per-Ake Larson*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 华为GaussDB数据库通过引入流水线执行、高效节点间数据洗牌及统一总线等技术优化，在30TB TPC-H基准测试中性能超越最佳公开结果40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升GaussDB在处理大规模和复杂分析型工作负载时的性能表现。

Method: 采用流水线执行模型；优化更快且更具可扩展性的节点间数据洗牌机制；利用统一总线和统一远程内存访问技术；扩展基于成本的Bloom过滤器放置支持并实现跨节点的Bloom过滤器流式策略。

Result: 在30 TB规模下，TPC-H工作负载的性能超过了此前发布的最佳结果40%。

Conclusion: 通过对分布式共享无架构进行关键系统级优化，显著提升了GaussDB在大型分析工作负载中的处理能力和效率。

Abstract: GaussDB is Huawei's premier database system, designed for large-scale deployments and the most demanding workloads. It is a distributed shared-nothing system, capable of handling all types of workloads. This paper outlines a series of modifications to GaussDB aimed at improving its performance on large-scale and complex analytical workloads. After these changes, its performance on the TPC-H workload exceeded the best published result by 40% at 30 TB.
  The key enhancements to achieve this elite performance include adopting a pipeline execution model, a faster and more scalable inter-node data shuffle, exploiting a unified bus and unified remote memory access. We also expanded the support of cost-based Bloom filter placement and implemented several Bloom filter streaming strategies, enabling their use across nodes.

</details>
